FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 144 GLH 2022
Dated:10-8-2022 regarding altering the limits of Chikkamyageri village of
Yelburga Taluk and Koppal District and constituting a new revenue village called
Venkateshanagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
144 GLH 2022 Dated:17-8-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:17-
10-2022 and whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

(4587)

QBSS BOF LE TW, Aerdawd, O&, Awe, 9092 een YA
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Chikkamyageri village of Yelburga taluk in Koppal District to form a new revenue
village called Venkateshanagar of Yelburga taluk Koppal district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 47 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:20/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Chikkanagavalli village in Mandikallu Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Chikkanagavalli village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in column (3) of the table
shall be declared as habitation area of Chikkanagavalli village of Chikkaballapura
Taluk of Chikkaballapura District convert the same to form extension within the
boundary of Chikkanagavalli revenue village and same will be herein after be named
as Chikkanagavalli village in Chikkaballapura Taluk Chikkaballapur district.

located is proposed to be extended as part of original gramatana extent, habitation area
(Gramatana), of Chikkanagavalli village of Mandikallu Hobli, Chikkaballapura
Taluk of Chikkaballapura District.

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under
section5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964)

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 23 PFH 2023 Karanataka Government Secretaeiat
Multi Store Building
Bengaluru,Date:20-02-2023

PERLIMENTRY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred By Section 5 of The Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka Act NO 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified clauses(i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population area of the village the demand under land revenue In the village
and having regard to the administrative convenience , the Government Of the
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Chithaganahalli
village of Pavagada taluk Tumkur District and to constitute a new village called
Dyavayyanapalya as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers
and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercises of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice Is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the notification in the Official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed
to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumkur District,Tumkur.

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table bellow and areas specified in column (3) there of
shall be exclude from limits of said village of Pavagada taluk in Tumkur District to
form a new Revenue village called as Dyavayyana palya of Pavagada taluk Tumkur
district along with the survey numbers included in column (4)there of.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 51 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:20/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Hanumanthapur village in Mandikallu Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Hanumanthapur village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said
revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in column (3) of the table
shall be declared as habitation area of Hanumanthapur village of Chikkaballapura
Taluk of Chikkaballapura District convert the same to form extension within the
boundary of Hanumanthapur revenue village and same will be herein after be named
as Hanumanthapur village in Chikkaballapura Taluk Chikkaballapur district.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 321 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:20-02-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 321 PFH 2022 Dated:05-11-2022
regarding altering the limits of Haranahalli village of Arasikere Taluk and Hassan
District and constituting a new revenue village called Yarehalli, Kankanchenahalli,
Hanumathanagara, was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD
321 PFH 2022 Dated:07-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:07-11-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Haranahalli taluk Arasikere in Hassan District to form a new revenue
village called Yarehalli of Arsikere taluk Hassan district along with survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

RD 46 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:20/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Harobande village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura
District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing revenue village. The
area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Harobande village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Harobande village of
Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Harobande Revenue village and same
will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Harobande village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

located is proposed to be extended as part of original gramatana extent, habitation area
(Gramatana), of Harobande village of Kasaba Hobli, Chikkaballapura Taluk of
Chikkaballapura District.

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under

persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication

village Hirebelaguli of Holenarasipura taluk in Hassan District to form a new

with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 49 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:20/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Kalavara village in Nandi Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura
District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing revenue village. The
area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Kalavara village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in column (3) of the table
shall be declared as habitation area of Kalavara village of Chikkaballapura Taluk of
Chikkaballapura District convert the same to form extension within the boundary of
Kalavara revenue village and same will be herein after be named as Kalavara village
in Chikkaballapura Taluk Chikkaballapur district.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 05(12) Kamgrara 2021
Dated:05-12-2022 regarding altering the limits of Kellengere village of Arasikere
Taluk and Hassan District and constituting a new revenue village called Vishnupura
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 05(12) kamgrara
2021 dated:06-12-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely
to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated
06-12-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Vishnupura taluk Arasikere in.District to form a new revenue village

called Kellengere of Arasikere taluk Hassan district along with survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 138 GLH 2022 Dated:13-
10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Kolakere (G) village of Davanagere District
and Channagiri Taluk and constituting a new revenue sub-village called
Malleshwara was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 138 GLH
2022 Dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely
to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:14-
10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act,1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely.

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in colum (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Kolkere village of Channagiri Taluk in Davanagere District to from a new revenue
village called as Malleshwara of Channagiri Taluk and Davanagere District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 53 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:20/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Kolavanahalli village in Nandi Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura
District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing revenue village. The
area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Kolavanahalli village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.

SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Kolavanahalli village of
Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Kolavanahalli Revenue village and
same will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Kolavanahalli village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

Chikkaballapura District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Lakkammanahalli of Chikkamagalur taluk Chikkamagalur District and to
constitute a new revenue village called Ajjanahatti as specified in schedule along
with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkamagalur District, Chikkamagalur

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Lakkammanahalli, Chikkamagalur taluk in Chikkamagalur District to
form a new revenue village called Ajjanahatti of Chikkamagalur taluk
Chikkamagalur district along with the survey numbers included in column (4)
thereof.

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 145 PFH 2022, Dated:05-12-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Kachigatta village of Arasikere Taluk and
Hassan District and constituting a new revenue village called Parvathipura was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 145 PFH 2022,
Dated:6-12-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:06-12-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

village of Kachigatta taluk Arasikere in Hassan District to form a new revenue
village called of Parvathipura taluk Arasikere in Hassan district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

RD 52 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:20/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Nallimaradahalli village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Nallimaradahalli village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said
revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Nallimaradahalli village

of Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Nallimaradahalli Revenue village and
same will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Nallimaradahalli village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

Hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the column(3) above with
survey numbers specified in column(2) in which the said unrecorded habitation is
located is proposed to be extended as part of original gramatana extent, habitation area
(Gramatana), of Nallimaradahalli village of Kasaba Hobli, Chikkaballapura Taluk
of Chikkaballapura District.

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under
section5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964)

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No.RD 137 GLH 2022 Dated:13-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Santhebennur (G) village of Channagiri Taluk
and Davanagere District and constituting a new revenue sub-village called Gedduru
And Vijayanagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
137 GLH 2022 Dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:14-
10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely.

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column(2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Santebennuru village of Channagiri Taluk , Davangere District to form a new
revenue village called as Gedduru of Channagiri Taluk, Davangere District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-362
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 54 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:20/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential

area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Soppahalli Majara Gandlahalli village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Soppahalli Majara Gandlahalli village for extension of original gramatana extent of
the said revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Soppahalli Majara
Gandlahalli village of Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert
the same to form habitation extension within the boundary of Soppahalli Majara
Gandlahalli Revenue village and same will be named as part of original gramatana
extent of Soppahalli Majara Gandlahalli village Chikkaballpura Taluk,
Chikkaballpura District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 182 PFH 2022 Dated:04-01-
2023 regarding altering the limits of Talluru village of Yelburga Taluk and Koppal
District and constituting a new revenue village called Shivapur was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 182 PFH 2022 dated:09-01-2023 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:15-01-2023 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Talluru village of Yelburga taluk in Koppal District to form a new revenue village
called Shivapur of Yelburga taluk Koppal district along with survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv} of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Aralikere Village of Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkur District to convert the existing
AralikerePalya unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the
schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Aralikere village for conversion of AralikerePalya unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Tumkur District.

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared ashabitation areaof AralikerePalya village of
TuruvekereTaluk of Tumkur District convert the same to form hamlet/extension
within the boundary of Aralikere Revenue village and same will herein after be
named as AralikerePalya of Aralikere villagein Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkur
District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no.12 of 1964 ) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Bandiwad village,
Hubli Taluk, Dharwad district and to constitute a new village called Hosa-Bandiwad
as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Dharwad District, Dharwad.

Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Hubli Taluk in Dharwad District to form a new revenue village called as
Hosa-Bandiwad of Hubli Taluk and Dharwad district, along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 57 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:24/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Doddakirugambi village in Nandi Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing

revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Doddakirugambi village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said
revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura district.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Doddakirugambi village
of Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Doddakirugambi Revenue village and
same will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Doddakirugambi village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

located is proposed to be extended as part of original gramatana extent, habitation area

of Chikkaballapura District.

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under
section5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964)

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Gantiganahalli village in Nandi Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura
District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing revenue village. The
area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Gantiganahalli village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura district.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Hanumathapura Village of Jagalur taluk Davanagere District and to constitute a
new village called Chitrahalli as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new survey numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Davanagere District, Davanagere.

Hanumathapura village of Jagalur taluk and Davanagere District to form a new

with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:24/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Jathavara Hosahalli village in Nandi Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Jathavara Hosahalli village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said
revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura district.

extension within the boundary of Jathavara Hosahalli Revenue village and same will be
named as part of original gramatana extent of Jathavara Hosahalli village

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Kallenahalli Village of Krishnarajanagar taluk Mysore District and to constitute a
new revenue village called Marigudikopalu and Hosakopalu as specified in
schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Mysore District, Mysore.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kallenahalli of Krishnarajanagar taluk in Mysore District to form a new
revenue village called Marigudikopalu of Krishnarajanagar taluk Mysore district
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 63 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:24-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kallakavatagi Village of
Tikota taluk Vijayapur District and to constitute a new revenue village called
Kasu Nagar, Kesu Nagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be
affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after
thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objections or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayapur
District, Vijayapur

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the
areas specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the
limits of said village Kallakavatagi of Tikota taluk in Vijayapur District to
form a new revenue village called Kasu Nagar of Tikota taluk Vijayapur
district along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 62 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:24-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Kambagi Village of Babaleshwar taluk Vijayapur District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Hanamasagar as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayapur District, Vijayapur

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kambagi of Babaleshwar taluk in Vijayapur District to form a new revenue
village called Hanamasagar of Babaleshwar taluk Vijayapur district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 55 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:24/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Keshavara village in Nandi Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura
District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing revenue village. The
area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Keshavara village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura district.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Keshavara village of
Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Keshavara Revenue village and same
will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Keshavara village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

survey numbers specified in column(2) in which the said unrecorded habitation is
located is proposed to be extended as part of original gramatana extent, habitation area
(Gramatana), of Keshavara village of Nandi Hobli, Chikkaballapura Taluk of
Chikkaballapura District.

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under

Bengaluru, Date:21-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no.12 of 1964 ) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kusugal village, Hubli
Taluk, Dharwad district and to constitute a new village called Sanna-Kusugal as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act,notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Dharwad District, Dharwad.

SCHEDULE - 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Hubli Taluk in Dharwad District to form a new revenue village called as
Sanna-Kusugal of Hubli Taluk and Dharwad district, along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

PR-377

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 59 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:24/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Manchanabale village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Manchanabale village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura district.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Manchanabale village of
Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Manchanabale Revenue village and
same will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Manchanabale village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

Hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the column(3) above with
survey numbers specified in column(2) in which the said unrecorded habitation is
located is proposed to be extended as part of original gramatana extent, habitation area
(Gramatana), of Manchanabale village of Kasaba Hobli, Chikkaballapura Taluk of
Chikkaballapura District.

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under
section5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964)

PR-378
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 22 PFH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:21-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the
limits of Mummigatti village of Dharawad Taluk in Dharwad District and to
constitute a new village called Renukanagar as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey and new numbers assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Dharwad District, Dharwad.

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Mummigatti of Dharwad Taluk in Dharwad District to form a new revenue
village called Renukanagar of DharwadTaluk in Dharwad District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof. thereof.

PR-379
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 58 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:24/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Nugitahalli village in Nandi Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura
District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing revenue village. The
area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Nugitahalli village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura district.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Nugitahalli village of
Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Nugitahalli Revenue village and same
will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Nugitahalli village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

Hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the column(3) above with
survey numbers specified in column(2) in which the said unrecorded habitation is
located is proposed to be extended as part of original gramatana extent, habitation area
(Gramatana), of Nugitahalli village of Nandi Hobli, Chikkaballapura Taluk of
Chikkaballapura District.

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under
section5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964)

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 340 PFH 2022
Dated:05-11-2022 regarding altering the limits of Tungabhadra village of Raichur
Taluk and Raichur District and constituting a new revenue village called Ramnagar
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 340 PFH 2022
Dated:08-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:17-11-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Raichur taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue village called
Ramnagar of Raichur taluk Raichur district along with survey numbers included in
column (4) thereof.

PR-381
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 17 PFH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:21-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Byranayakanahalli of Jagalur taluk Davanagere District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Rangaiahanahatti as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected

thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Davanagere District, Davanagere

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Byranayakanahalli of Jagalur taluk in Davanagere District to form a new
revenue village called Rangaiahanahatti of Jagalur taluk in Davanagere district
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.
